&-\frac{1}{192}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a \beta \gamma}W{}_{a b}(\Sigma{}^{c b}){}_{\beta}{}^{\delta}X{}_{\delta i}(\Gamma{}_{c}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\alpha} -\frac{1}{192}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a \beta \gamma}W{}_{a b}(\Sigma{}^{c b}){}^{\delta}{}_{\beta}X{}_{\delta i}(\Gamma{}_{c}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}\\
&-\frac{1}{512}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a}{}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}(\Sigma{}^{b c}){}^{\delta \rho}W{}^{\beta}{}_{\delta \rho i}W{}_{b c}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\alpha} +\frac{1}{1024}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a \beta \gamma}(\Sigma{}^{b c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\delta}X{}_{\delta i}W{}_{b c}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}\\
&+\frac{1}{1024}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a \beta \gamma}(\Sigma{}^{b c}){}^{\delta}{}_{\beta}X{}_{\delta i}W{}_{b c}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\alpha} -\frac{1}{512}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a}{}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}W{}^{b c}(\Sigma{}_{b c}){}^{\delta \rho}W{}^{\beta}{}_{\delta \rho i}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}\\
&+\frac{1}{1024}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a \beta \gamma}W{}^{b c}(\Sigma{}_{b c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\delta}X{}_{\delta i}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\alpha} +\frac{1}{1024}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal T{}^{a \beta \gamma}W{}^{b c}(\Sigma{}_{b c}){}^{\delta}{}_{\beta}X{}_{\delta i}(\Gamma{}_{a}){}_{\gamma}{}^{\alpha}\\
&-\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}W{}^{\alpha \beta}\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}{}_{a}{}^{b}\big(\nabla_{b}\lambda{}_{\beta i}\big) -\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha k}\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta k a}{}^{b}\varepsilon{}^{i j}\big(\nabla_{b}W{}^{\beta}{}_{\alpha}\big)\\
&-\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha k}\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta k a}{}^{b}\mathcal T{}_{b}{}^{\beta \gamma}\varepsilon{}^{i j}W{}_{\gamma \alpha} +\frac{i}{48}W^{-1}\lambda{}_{\alpha}{}^{i}\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta i a}{}^{b}\mathcal R(S){}_{b}{}^{\beta}{}_{j}{}^{\alpha j}\\
&+\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}W{}^{\alpha \beta}\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\alpha}{}^{i b}{}_{a}\big(\nabla_{b}\lambda{}_{\beta i}\big) +\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha k}\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta k}{}^{b}{}_{a}\varepsilon{}^{i j}\big(\nabla_{b}W{}^{\beta}{}_{\alpha}\big)\\

&-\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}W{}^{\alpha \beta}\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\alpha}{}^{i b c}(\Sigma{}_{b c}){}_{\beta}{}^{\gamma}\big(\nabla_{a}\lambda{}_{\gamma i}\big) -\frac{1}{48}W^{-1}\varepsilon{}_{i j}\lambda{}^{\alpha k}\mathcal R(M){}^{a}{}_{\beta k}{}^{b c}(\Sigma{}_{b c}){}_{\alpha}{}^{\gamma}\varepsilon{}^{i j}\big(\nabla_{a}W{}^{\beta}{}_{\gamma}\big)\\
